Motivation: 研究Tribonacci数列与Lucas数列乘积之间的数值关系，探索这两个重要数列之间的交叉性质，解决相关的丢番图方程问题

Method: 使用丢番图方程理论、数列性质分析、模运算和不等式估计等方法，通过分析数列的增长速度和模性质来确定方程的解

Result: 证明了方程T_k = L_mL_n仅有有限个正整数解，并确定了所有具体的解，包括平凡解和一些非平凡解

Conclusion: 成功解决了Tribonacci数与Lucas数乘积相等的丢番图方程，为数列交叉研究提供了新的结果，展示了组合数论方法在解决此类问题中的有效性

Abstract: Let $T_{k}$ be the $k^{\textrm{th}}$ Tribonacci number and $L_{n}$ be the $n^{\textrm{th}}$ Lucas number defined by their respective recurrence relation $T_{k}=T_{k-1}+T_{k-2}+T_{k-3}$ and $L_{n}=L_{n-1}+L_{n-2}$. In this study, we solve the Diophantine equation $T_{k} = L_{m}L_{n}$ for positive integer unknowns $m$, $n$, and $k$ and prove our results.

</details>


### [144] [$L$-functions and linear periods for $\mathbf{GL}_4 \times \mathbf{GL}_2$ and $\mathbf{GU}_{2,2}\times \mathbf{GL}_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14586)
*Antonio Cauchi,Armando Gutierrez Terradillos*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文给出了$\wedge^2 \otimes \mathrm{std}_2$ $L$-函数的新积分表示，并利用theta对应建立了中心$L$-值与周期积分的关系，为Wan-Zhang猜想提供了新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究$\mathbf{GU}_{2,2}\times \mathbf{GL}_2$和$\mathbf{GL}_4 \times \mathbf{GL}_2$上尖点形式的$L$-函数表示，探索中心$L$-值与周期积分的关系，验证Wan-Zhang猜想。

Method: 1. 构造$\wedge^2 \otimes \mathrm{std}_2$ $L$-函数的新积分表示；2. 利用theta对应连接$(\mathbf{GL}_4,\mathbf{GL}_4)$；3. 建立强球面对$(\mathbf{GL}_4 \times \mathbf{GL}_2,\mathbf{GL}_2 \times \mathbf{GL}_2)$的中心$L$-值与周期积分的关系。

Result: 1. 获得了$L$-函数的新积分表示；2. 证明了中心$L$-值与$\mathbf{GL}_2 \times_{\mathrm{det}} \mathbf{GL}_2$上的非球面周期之间的关系；3. 在处处非分歧情况下为Wan-Zhang猜想提供了新证据。

Conclusion: 该工作通过构造新积分表示和利用theta对应，建立了$L$-函数中心值与周期积分的精确关系，为相关数论猜想提供了重要支持，特别是在处处非分歧情况下验证了Wan-Zhang猜想。

Abstract: We give a new integral representation of the $\wedge^2 \otimes \mathrm{std}_2$ $L$-function of generic cusp forms on $\mathbf{GU}_{2,2}\times \mathbf{GL}_2$ and $\mathbf{GL}_4 \times \mathbf{GL}_2$ and we use it to prove a relation between its central $L$-value and the non-spherical period over $\mathbf{GL}_2 \times_{\mathrm{det}} \mathbf{GL}_2$. Exploiting the theta correspondence for $(\mathbf{GL}_4,\mathbf{GL}_4)$, we obtain a relation between the central value of the $L$-function attached to the strongly tempered spherical pair $(\mathbf{GL}_4 \times \mathbf{GL}_2,\mathbf{GL}_2 \times \mathbf{GL}_2)$ and its associated period. In the case of cusp forms on $\mathbf{GL}_4 \times \mathbf{GL}_2$ that are unramified everywhere, our formula gives new evidence towards a conjecture of Wan-Zhang.

</details>


### [145] [Completing the classification of torsion subgroups for rational elliptic curves over sextic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14718)
*Nikola Adžaga,Tomislav Gužvić*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文完成了六次数域上椭圆曲线有理点挠子群的完全分类，证明了C₃⊕C₁₈挠子群不存在


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完成六次数域上椭圆曲线有理点挠子群的完全分类，解决先前工作中唯一未确定的挠子群C₃⊕C₁₈的存在性问题

Method: 利用椭圆曲线的Galois表示理论，将C₃⊕C₁₈挠子群的存在性转化为模2和3-adic Galois表示的约束条件，再通过椭圆曲线在ℚ上的ℓ-adic Galois像的分类，将算术约束转化为丢番图几何问题，最后分析模曲线上的有理点

Result: 证明了C₃⊕C₁₈挠子群在六次数域上的椭圆曲线中不存在，从而完成了六次数域上椭圆曲线挠子群的完全分类

Conclusion: 六次数域上椭圆曲线的有理点挠子群分类现已完全确定，所有可能的挠子群类型都已明确，C₃⊕C₁₈不在其中

Abstract: We complete the classification of torsion subgroups $E(K)_{\text{tors}}$ that can occur for an elliptic curve $E/\mathbb{Q}$ over a sextic number field $K$. Previous work determined the complete set of these groups, leaving the existence of only one group in question: $C_3 \oplus C_{18}$. We prove that this group does not occur.
  Our proof relies on the theory of Galois representations attached to elliptic curves. The assumed existence of a $C_3 \oplus C_{18}$ torsion subgroup would impose strong, simultaneous constraints on the mod-$2$ and $3$-adic Galois representations of the curve. By applying the recent classification of $\ell$-adic Galois images for elliptic curves over $\mathbb{Q}$, we translate these arithmetic constraints into a problem of Diophantine geometry: the $j$-invariant of such a curve must correspond to a rational point on one of the finitely many modular curves. We then analyze these curves using classical methods and show that none have the necessary rational points corresponding to elliptic curves without complex multiplication, thereby proving our main result.

</details>


### [146] [Linear independence of theta series of positive-definite, unimodular even lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14746)
*Manuel K. -H. Müller*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了正定偶幺模格theta级数的线性独立性，证明了对于秩m≥24的格，使theta级数线性独立的最小次数g满足m/2 ≤ g ≤ 3m/4


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正定偶幺模格的theta级数的线性独立性，这类格在数论和编码理论中有重要应用。了解theta级数何时线性独立有助于理解格的结构和性质。

Method: 通过分析正定偶幺模格的theta级数，利用格理论和模形式理论的方法，推导出线性独立性所需的最小次数g的上下界。

Result: 对于秩m≥24的正定偶幺模格，使theta级数线性独立的最小次数g满足不等式：m/2 ≤ g ≤ 3m/4。这给出了g的明确上下界。

Conclusion: 论文确定了正定偶幺模格theta级数线性独立所需最小次数的范围，为格理论和模形式理论提供了新的界限结果。

Abstract: We show that the minimal $g$ for which the degree $g$ theta series of positive-definite, unimodular even lattices of rank $m\geq24$ are linearly independent is bounded between $\frac{m}{2}$ and $\frac{3m}{4}$.

</details>


### [147] [The Tetrahedral (or $6j$) Symbol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14908)
*Akshay Venkatesh,X. Griffin Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文将6j符号推广到局部域上三元正交群SO₃的不可约表示标记的四面体，构造了一个标量不变量，并建立了与Spin₁₂的Weyl群对称性和相对朗兰兹对偶的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Racah、Wigner和Regge发展的6j符号理论推广到更一般的代数结构，研究局部域上三元正交群SO₃表示标记的四面体的不变量，并探索其与高维李群对称性和朗兰兹对偶的联系。

Method: 为SO₃不可约表示标记的四面体构造标量不变量，提供多种计算公式（包括超几何型积分和函数），证明该不变量具有Spin₁₂的23040元Weyl群对称性，并通过相对朗兰兹对偶解释结果，其中对偶故事来自Spin₁₂在16维旋量锥上的作用。

Result: 成功构造了6j符号的推广不变量，建立了其与Spin₁₂ Weyl群对称性的联系，并通过相对朗兰兹对偶框架提供了代数几何解释，将不变量与Spin₁₂在旋量锥上的作用联系起来。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典6j符号理论推广到局部域上的三元正交群表示，揭示了高维李群对称性在量子不变量理论中的作用，并通过朗兰兹对偶建立了不同数学领域之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We will attach a scalar invariant to a tetrahedron whose edges are labelled by irreducible representations of a ternary orthogonal group $\mathrm{SO}_3$ over a local field. This generalizes the $6j$ symbol whose theory was developed by Racah, Wigner, and Regge.
  We give several formulas for this invariant, including in terms of hypergeometric-type integrals and functions, and show that it admits a symmetry by the the $23040$-element Weyl group of $\mathrm{Spin}_{12}$. We then interpret these results in terms of relative Langlands duality, where the dual story comes from the action of $\mathrm{Spin}_{12}$ on a $16$-dimensional cone of spinors.

</details>

### [148] [Spatiotemporal noise stabilizes unbounded diversity in strongly-competitive communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13423)
*Amer Al-Hiyasat,Daniel W. Swartz,Jeff Gore,Mehran Kardar*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 空间结构和环境噪声共同作用可解决生物多样性-稳定性悖论，允许任意多物种在强竞争下稳定共存


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典生态模型预测大型多样化群落不稳定，这与自然界观察到的稳定生物多样性相矛盾，需要解决这一长期存在的多样性-稳定性悖论

Method: 扩展广义Lotka-Volterra模型，纳入空间结构和跨时空的环境波动，分析空间与环境噪声的联合效应

Result: 单独的空间结构或环境噪声无法解决多样性-稳定性矛盾，但两者结合可使任意多物种在强竞争下稳定共存；时空噪声驱动丰度波动的异常标度（泰勒定律），在群落层面表现为有效亚线性自抑制作用

Conclusion: 时空噪声为多样性-稳定性悖论提供了新颖解决方案，是复杂群落能够持续存在的通用机制，在高多样性极限下使群落稳定且渐近中性

Abstract: Classical ecological models predict that large, diverse communities should be unstable, presenting a central challenge to explaining the stable biodiversity seen in nature. We revisit this long-standing problem by extending the generalized Lotka-Volterra model to include both spatial structure and environmental fluctuations across space and time. We find that neither space nor environmental noise alone can resolve the tension between diversity and stability, but that their combined effects permit arbitrarily many species to stably coexist despite strongly disordered competitive interactions. We analytically characterize the noise-induced transition to coexistence, showing that spatiotemporal noise drives an anomalous scaling of abundance fluctuations, known empirically as Taylor's law. At the community level, this manifests as an effective sublinear self-inhibition that renders the community stable and asymptotically neutral in the high-diversity limit. Spatiotemporal noise thus provides a novel resolution to the diversity-stability paradox and a generic mechanism by which complex communities can persist.

</details>


### [149] [Bistability to Quad-stability: Emergence of Hybrid Phenotypes & Enhanced Spatio-temporal Plasticity in Presence of Host-Circuit Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13630)
*Ranu Kundu,Priya Chakraborty,Sohini Guin,Shyam Sundar Poriah,Sayantari Ghosh*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了多稳态疾病（如癌症）中，生长反馈引起的资源竞争与空间扩散如何共同驱动时空可塑性和表型异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在癌症等多稳态疾病中，时空可塑性对表型变异谱系至关重要。虽然反应扩散框架在发育生物学中广泛应用，但资源竞争与生长反馈对空间组织的协同效应研究较少。研究者旨在理解这些因素如何共同影响细胞行为和表型异质性。

Method: 采用反应扩散框架，研究双稳态遗传电路在高资源竞争条件下的行为。分析生长反馈、空间扩散和内在非线性之间的相互作用，特别关注固定点相对稳定性、扩散率和扩散不对称性等参数的影响。

Result: 观察到：1）高资源竞争下的双稳态遗传电路产生多种涌现表型，类似于癌症系统；2）空间扩散与内在非线性耦合驱动随时间演化的不同空间动力学；3）时空可塑性受固定点相对稳定性、扩散率和扩散不对称性调控。

Conclusion: 生长诱导的资源竞争与扩散相结合为理解转移和癌症进展提供了更深入的见解。这种机制框架有助于解释癌症系统中的表型异质性和时空可塑性。

Abstract: In the context of multistability driven diseases, like cancer, spatiotemporal plasticity plays a significant role to achieve a spectrum of phenotypic variations. The interplay between gene regulatory networks and environmental factors, such as resource competition and spatial diffusion, plays a crucial role in determining cellular behaviour and phenotypic heterogeneity. Though reaction diffusion frameworks have been widely applied in developmental biology, less attention has been paid to the simultaneous effects of resource competition and growth feedback on spatial organization. In this paper, we observed that a bistable genetic circuit under high resource competition due to growth feedback gives rise to multiple emergent phenotypes, as observed in cancer systems. Furthermore, we observed how spatial diffusion coupled with intrinsic nonlinearity can drive the emergence of distinct spatial dynamics over time. The observed spatiotemporal plasticity can also be driven by the comparative stability of the fixed points, diffusivity, and asymmetry of diffusion. Our findings highlight that growth-induced resource competition combined with diffusion can provide deeper insights into metastasis and cancer progression.

</details>


### [150] [Gauge-Mediated Contagion: A Quantum Electrodynamics-Inspired Framework for Non-Local Epidemic Dynamics and Superdiffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13913)
*Jose de Jesus Bernal-Alvarado,David Delepine*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 提出基于量子电动力学（QED）的规范场介导流行病学模型，用病原体场φ取代经典SIR模型的直接接触范式，通过积分掉病原体场自然获得流行病的非局域特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SIR模型基于直接接触范式，无法充分描述流行病的非局域特性。本文旨在建立一种基于规范场理论的流行病学模型，将环境（病原体场）作为基本要素纳入动力学，从而更自然地描述流行病传播的非局域特征。

Method: 采用Doi-Peliti形式体系推导系统的有效作用量，建立标准费曼规则用于微扰计算。利用标准QED技术，将重整化病原体质量、德拜屏蔽等概念与流行病学概念关联。在模型中引入规范场介导的相互作用，通过积分掉病原体场获得非局域特性。

Result: 计算得到一阶有效再生数R_eff，并证明流行病发生的条件与病原体质量的相变相关。展示了如何在该形式体系中轻松纳入超级传播者。建立了流行病学参数与量子场论概念之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 规范场介导的流行病学模型为理解流行病传播提供了新的理论框架，能够自然地描述非局域特性，并将流行病学概念与量子场论中的相变、重整化等概念联系起来，为流行病动力学研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a gauge-mediated Epidemiological Model inspired by Quantum Electrodynamics (QED). In this model, the ``direct contact'' paradigm of classical SIR models is replaced by a gauge-mediated interaction where the environment, represented by a pathogen field $\varphi$, plays a fundamental role in the epidemic dynamics. In this model, the non-local characteristics of epidemics appear naturally by integrating out the pathogen field. Utilizing the Doi-Peliti formalism, we derive the effective action of the system and the standard Feynman rules that can be used to compute perturbatively any observables. Using standard QED techniques, we show how to relate renormalized pathogen mass, Debye screening, to epidemiological concepts and we compute at first order the effective reproductive number,$R_{eff}$, and how the condition to have an epidemic is related to a phase transition in the pathogen mass. We show that the superspreading hosts can be included easily in this formalism.

</details>


### [151] [Conditions for Bacterial Selection and Extinction Driven by Growth-Kill Trade-Off in Cyclic Antimicrobial Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14645)
*Nerea Martínez-López,Niclas Nordholt,Frank Schreiber,Míriam R. García*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 提出最小种群动力学模型研究周期性抗菌应用中的细菌选择，基于生长和杀灭速率预测抗菌治疗成功与否


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗菌方案是预防病原菌传播的主要手段，但细菌会发展出降低抗菌敏感性的机制，导致治疗失败并选择出耐药或耐受菌株。需要理解周期性抗菌应用中细菌选择的动态机制。

Method: 提出最小种群动力学模型，将细菌选择建模为生存优势（抗菌杀灭减少）与潜在适应度代价（生长速率降低）之间的权衡。模型基于两个关键细菌性状：生长速率和杀灭速率。

Result: 模型推导出基于细菌生长和杀灭速率确定周期性抗菌治疗成功与否的有用表达式。这些结果可直接应用于预防实际方案中耐药和耐受菌株的选择和传播。

Conclusion: 该最小模型为理解和预测周期性抗菌方案中的细菌选择提供了理论框架，有助于设计更有效的抗菌策略以防止耐药和耐受菌株的出现和传播。

Abstract: Antimicrobial protocols - using substances such as antibiotics or disinfectants - remain the preferred option for preventing the spread of pathogenic bacteria. However, bacteria can develop mechanisms to reduce their antimicrobial susceptibility, which can lead to treatment failure and the selection of resistance or tolerance. In this work, we propose a minimal population dynamics model to study bacterial selection during cyclic antimicrobial application, a commonly used protocol. Selection in bacterial populations with heterogeneous antimicrobial susceptibility is modelled here as a trade-off between survival advantage (reduction in antimicrobial killing) and potential fitness costs (reduction in growth rate) of the less susceptible strains. The proposed model allows us to derive useful expressions for determining the success of cyclic antimicrobial treatments based on two bacterial traits: growth and kill rates. The results obtained here are directly applicable to preventing the selection and spread of resistant and tolerant bacterial strains in real-life protocols.

</details>


### [152] [Quasilocalization under coupled mutation-selection dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14863)
*C. J. Palpal-latoc,Ian Vega*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个简单通用关系，将准物种的Hill多样性指数与有效适应度方差与平均突变率平方的比值联系起来，该比值称为定位因子，可用于预测准物种在突变率较高时的定位程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当突变率较高时，准物种理论或Eigen模型预测种群中最适应的类型可能不会占主导地位。超过临界突变率后，种群甚至可能完全从适应度景观的峰值中脱离，最适应的类型反而会丢失。虽然已有大量研究试图理解这一特殊情景，但通常没有简单的方法可以预测任意适应度景观和突变率下的最终定位程度。

Method: 推导了一个简单通用的关系，将准物种的Hill多样性指数（生态学中的多样性度量）与有效适应度方差与平均突变率平方的比值联系起来。这个比值被称为定位因子，是从分解的惊奇度或随机熵变化率的均值近似中产生的。

Result: 获得了一个关系式，定义了Hill多样性指数的组合，可以补充其他用于真实病毒准物种的复杂性或多样性度量。其优势在于在Eigen模型下有潜在的生物学解释。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个简单而通用的框架，通过定位因子来预测准物种在突变率较高时的定位程度，为病毒准物种的多样性分析提供了新的理论工具和生物学解释。

Abstract: When mutations are rampant, quasispecies theory or Eigen's model predicts that the fittest type in a population may not dominate. Beyond a critical mutation rate, the population may even be delocalized completely from the peak of the fitness landscape and the fittest is ironically lost. Extensive efforts have been made to understand this exceptional scenario. But in general, there is no simple prescription that predicts the eventual degree of localization for arbitrary fitness landscapes and mutation rates. Here, we derive a simple and general relation linking the quasispecies' Hill numbers, which are diversity metrics in ecology, and the ratio of an effective fitness variance to the mean mutation rate squared. This ratio, which we call the localization factor, emerges from mean approximations of decomposed surprisal or stochastic entropy change rates. On the side of application, the relation we obtained here defines a combination of Hill numbers that may complement other complexity or diversity measures for real viral quasispecies. Its advantage being that there is an underlying biological interpretation under Eigen's model.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Dynamic Analysis and Optimal Prevention Strategies for Monkeypox Spread Modeled via the Mittag--Leffler Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13208)
*Mine Yurtoğlu,Dilara Yapışkan,Ebenezer Bonyah,Beyza Billur İskender Eroğlu,Derya Avcı,Delfim F. M. Torres*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究使用Atangana-Baleanu算子建模猴痘传播，分析疫苗接种、治疗和隔离三种控制策略的最优组合，发现三策略同时应用效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 猴痘已成为全球威胁，特别是非洲国家。天花根除后缺乏疫苗接种导致猴痘广泛传播。数学流行病学研究对临床研究具有补充作用，需要确定最优控制措施以减少暴露和感染人群并最小化成本。

Method: 使用Atangana-Baleanu算子建立猴痘传播模型，分析疫苗接种、治疗和隔离三种控制策略。对受控模型进行解的存在唯一性、稳定性和敏感性分析。采用Adams型预测-校正方法数值求解最优系统，分别评估单策略、双策略和三策略组合的效果。

Result: 数值模拟表明，同时应用疫苗接种、治疗和隔离三种控制策略是实现目标结果的最有效策略。研究确定了最优控制措施，能够有效减少暴露和感染个体率，同时最小化成本。

Conclusion: 疫苗接种、治疗和隔离的综合应用是控制猴痘疫情的最有效方法。该研究为公共卫生决策提供了数学依据，强调了多管齐下的防控策略在应对猴痘威胁中的重要性。

Abstract: Monkeypox is a viral disease belonging to the smallpox family. Although it has milder symptoms than smallpox in humans, it has become a global threat in recent years, especially in African countries. Initially, incidental immunity against monkeypox was provided by smallpox vaccines. However, the eradication of smallpox over time and thus the lack of vaccination has led to the widespread and clinical importance of monkeypox. Although mathematical epidemiology research on the disease is complementary to clinical studies, it has attracted attention in the last few years. The present study aims to discuss the indispensable effects of three control strategies such as vaccination, treatment, and quarantine to prevent the monkeypox epidemic modeled via the Atangana--Baleanu operator. The main purpose is to determine optimal control measures planned to reduce the rates of exposed and infected individuals at the minimum costs. For the controlled model, the existence-uniqueness of the solutions, stability, and sensitivity analysis, and numerical optimal solutions are exhibited. The optimal system is numerically solved using the Adams-type predictor--corrector method. In the numerical simulations, the efficacy of the vaccination, treatment, and quarantine controls is evaluated in separate analyzes as single-, double-, and triple-control strategies. The results demonstrate that the most effective strategy for achieving the aimed outcome is the simultaneous application of vaccination, treatment, and quarantine controls.

</details>


### [154] [Stochastic variance reduced extragradient methods for solving hierarchical variational inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13510)
*Pavel Dvurechensky,Andrea Ebner,Johannes Carl Schnebel,Shimrit Shtern,Mathias Staudigl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文首次证明了方差缩减随机算法在欧几里得和Bregman设置下求解分层变分不等式问题的收敛率和复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分不等式（VI）是一个广泛的问题类，包含函数最小化、鞍点问题、纳什均衡等特例。研究分层结构和有限和表示的光滑算子的VI优化问题具有重要理论价值和应用意义。

Method: 针对具有两层分层结构和每层光滑算子有限和表示的VI问题，提出了方差缩减随机算法，在欧几里得和Bregman两种设置下进行分析。

Result: 首次证明了方差缩减随机算法在求解分层变分不等式时的收敛率和复杂度结果，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Conclusion: 该工作为分层变分不等式问题提供了首个收敛性理论保证，扩展了随机优化方法在复杂分层结构问题中的应用范围。

Abstract: We are concerned with optimization in a broad sense through the lens of solving variational inequalities (VIs) -- a class of problems that are so general that they cover as particular cases minimization of functions, saddle-point (minimax) problems, Nash equilibrium problems, and many others. The key challenges in our problem formulation are the two-level hierarchical structure and finite-sum representation of the smooth operators in each level. For this setting, we are the first to prove convergence rates and complexity statements for variance-reduced stochastic algorithms approaching the solution of hierarchical VIs in Euclidean and Bregman setups.

</details>


### [155] [On the Existence of Periodic Solutions with Applications to Extremum-Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13615)
*Iasson Karafyllis,Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提供了两个用于研究动力系统周期解存在性和稳定性的结果：第一个建立了标量周期系统周期解与有界解的等价性；第二个给出了周期解存在和稳定的充分条件，并应用于极值搜索问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动力系统周期解的存在性和稳定性是动力系统理论中的核心问题。传统方法通常依赖于平均化定理和奇异摄动理论，但本文旨在提供不依赖这些工具的替代方法，特别是在极值搜索控制领域。

Method: 1. 针对标量周期系统，建立了周期解存在性与有界解存在性的等价关系。2. 针对一般周期动力系统，提出了周期解存在和稳定性的充分条件。3. 将理论结果应用于静态输出映射的极值搜索问题，避免了平均化定理和奇异摄动论证。

Result: 1. 证明了标量周期系统中周期解存在当且仅当存在有界解。2. 建立了周期解存在和稳定性的充分条件。3. 在极值搜索控制中获得了非局部结果，无需传统平均化方法。

Conclusion: 论文提供了研究周期解存在性和稳定性的新理论工具，特别适用于极值搜索控制问题。这些结果避免了传统平均化方法的局限性，为分析周期动力系统提供了更直接的途径。

Abstract: This paper provides two results that are useful in the study of the existence and the stability properties of a periodic solution for a given dynamical system. The first result deals with scalar time-periodic systems and establishes the equivalence of the existence of a periodic solution and the existence of a bounded solution. The second result provides sufficient conditions for the existence and the stability of a periodic solution for a time-periodic dynamical system. Both results are applied to extremum seeking problems for a static output map with no plant dynamics and novel non-local results are provided without the use of averaging theorems and singular perturbation arguments.

</details>


### [156] [An adaptive framework for first-order gradient methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13620)
*Xiaozhe Hu,Sara Pollock,Zhongqin Xue,Yunrong Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出自适应梯度方法框架，无需强凸参数先验知识，通过残差比几何平均估计收敛率上界，动态调整步长和动量参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中强凸参数难以估计，即使使用最优参数收敛速度仍可能较慢，需要无需先验知识的自适应参数调整方法。

Method: 使用连续残差范数比值的几何平均作为收敛率上界的经验估计，基于此自适应更新步长和动量参数，仅需少量额外计算。

Result: 自适应算法在二次优化问题中收敛速度至少与梯度下降相当；数值实验表明其性能与使用最优参数的算法相当，在某些情况下因捕捉局部信息而表现更优。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应梯度方法框架简单易实现且高效，无需强凸参数先验知识，在二次和非线性问题上均表现良好，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Gradient methods are widely used in optimization problems. In practice, while the smoothness parameter can be estimated utilizing techniques such as backtracking, estimating the strong convexity parameter remains a challenge; moreover, even with the optimal parameter choice, convergence can be slow. In this work, we propose a framework for dynamically adapting the step size and momentum parameters in first-order gradient methods for the optimization problem, without prior knowledge of the strong convexity parameter. The main idea is to use the geometric average of the ratios of successive residual norms as an empirical estimate of the upper bound on the convergence rate, which in turn allows us to adaptively update the algorithm parameters. The resulting algorithms are simple to implement, yet efficient in practice, requiring only a few additional computations on existing information. The proposed adaptive gradient methods are shown to converge at least as fast as gradient descent for quadratic optimization problems. Numerical experiments on both quadratic and nonlinear problems validate the effectiveness of the proposed adaptive algorithms. The results show that the adaptive algorithms are comparable to their counterparts using optimal parameters, and in some cases, they capture local information and exhibit improved performance.

</details>


### [157] [From time series to dissipativity of linear systems with dynamic supply rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13654)
*Henk J. van Waarde,Jeremy Coulson,Alberto Padoan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 基于行为系统理论，利用输入输出数据验证线性时不变系统的耗散性，通过二次差分形式表达耗散性条件，在系统可控且已知滞后上界的假设下，给出了数据信息性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统耗散性验证需要已知系统模型，而本文旨在直接从输入输出数据验证耗散性，避免系统辨识的完整过程，为数据驱动的控制理论提供新方法。

Method: 采用行为系统理论和二次差分形式(QDFs)框架，在系统可控且已知滞后上界的假设下，推导数据信息性的充分条件，特别针对静态供给率类给出充要条件。

Result: 建立了数据信息性对耗散性验证的充分条件，对于静态供给率类，这些条件也是必要的，且耗散性验证仅当数据能唯一辨识系统时才可能实现。

Conclusion: 行为系统理论与QDFs为数据驱动的耗散性验证提供了有效框架，揭示了数据信息性与系统可辨识性之间的紧密联系，特别在静态供给率情况下达到理论完备性。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of verifying dissipativity of linear time-invariant (LTI) systems using input-output data. We leverage behavioral systems theory to express dissipativity in terms of quadratic difference forms (QDFs), allowing the study of general dynamic quadratic supply rates. We work under the assumptions that the data-generating system is controllable, and an upper bound is given on its lag. As our main results, we provide sufficient conditions for the data to be informative for dissipativity. We also show that for a specific class of static supply rates, these conditions are both necessary and sufficient. For the latter supply rates, it turns out that certification of dissipativity is only possible from data that enable unique system identification. As auxiliary results, we highlight some properties of QDFs, such as upper bounds on the degree of storage functions.

</details>


### [158] [Persistent homology-based explicit topological control for 2D topology optimization with MMA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13856)
*Gengchen Li,Depeng Gao,Wenliang Yin,Hongwei Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种将持久同调集成到经典最小柔顺性拓扑优化中的显式可微拓扑控制框架，能够精确调控结构中的孔洞数量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制结构复杂性（特别是孔洞数量）是拓扑优化的基本挑战，现有方法依赖间接策略（如滤波技术、最小长度尺度控制或特定水平集初始化），这些方法仅隐式影响拓扑，无法精确调控拓扑特征

Method: 使用非均匀有理B样条表示设计域和密度场，采用持久图严格定量表征拓扑特征；给定预设孔洞数量，从持久对构建可微拓扑感知目标函数，并将其集成到柔顺性目标中，形成统一优化公式；使用移动渐近线法高效求解

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法能够实现对结构连通性和孔洞数量的显式控制，为拓扑调控提供了系统且数学基础扎实的策略

Conclusion: 通过将持久同调集成到拓扑优化中，提出了一种显式、可微的拓扑控制框架，能够精确调控结构中的孔洞数量，解决了现有方法无法直接控制拓扑特征的局限性

Abstract: Controlling structural complexity, particularly the number of holes, remains a fundamental challenge in topology optimization, with significant implications for both theoretical analysis and manufacturability. Most existing approaches rely on indirect strategies, such as filtering techniques, minimum length-scale control, or specific level-set initializations, which influence topology only implicitly and do not allow precise regulation of topological features. In this work, we propose an explicit and differentiable topology-control framework by integrating persistent homology into the classical minimum-compliance topology optimization problem. The design domain and density field are represented using non-uniform rational B-splines (NURBS), while persistence diagrams are employed to rigorously and quantitatively characterize topological features. Given a prescribed number of holes, a differentiable topology-aware objective is constructed from the persistence pairs and incorporated into the compliance objective, leading to a unified optimization formulation. The resulting problem is efficiently solved using the method of moving asymptotes (MMA).Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach enables explicit control over structural connectivity and the number of holes, thereby providing a systematic and mathematically grounded strategy for topology regulation.

</details>


### [159] [The local minimality of differentiable functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13965)
*Tien-Son Pham*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了光滑无约束优化中局部极小点的稳定性条件，基于Łojasiewicz不等式给出了必要和充分条件，并推导出局部极小性质可由特定阶数泰勒多项式确定的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光滑无约束优化中，理解局部极小点的稳定性条件至关重要。传统方法通常依赖于二阶条件（如Hessian矩阵正定性），但需要更一般的理论框架来刻画局部极小的稳定性特征，特别是当高阶导数发挥作用时。

Method: 采用Łojasiewicz不等式作为分析工具，建立局部极小点稳定性的必要和充分条件。通过分析函数在临界点附近的行为，推导出局部极小性质可由特定阶数泰勒多项式确定的充分条件。

Result: 获得了基于Łojasiewicz不等式的局部极小点稳定性判据，包括必要条件和充分条件。特别地，证明了在某些条件下，函数的局部极小性质完全由其在临界点的特定阶数泰勒多项式决定。

Conclusion: Łojasiewicz不等式为分析光滑无约束优化中局部极小点的稳定性提供了有效的理论框架。所得结果扩展了传统的二阶条件，为高阶优化问题的稳定性分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: In this paper we present necessary and sufficient conditions (in terms of Łojasiewicz inequalities) for the stability of local minimum points in smooth unconstrained optimization. In particular, we derive a sufficient condition for which the local minimum property of a given function is determined by its Taylor polynomial of a certain degree.

</details>


### [160] [A Homogeneous Second-Order Descent Ascent Algorithm for Nonconvex-Strongly Concave Minimax Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14058)
*Jia-Hao Chen,Zi Xu,Hui-Ling Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种新颖的齐次二阶下降上升（HSDA）算法，用于非凸-强凹极小极大优化问题，通过求解齐次特征值子问题获得搜索方向，在近正半定Hessian区域仍能保证充分负曲率，达到最优的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-3/2})$迭代复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非凸-强凹极小极大优化问题，现有二阶方法在Hessian矩阵接近正半定时难以有效逃离鞍点。需要设计一种能够在近正半定Hessian区域仍能保证充分负曲率下降方向的算法，以提升收敛效率和稳定性。

Method: 提出齐次二阶下降上升（HSDA）算法：1）每次迭代通过求解齐次特征值子问题计算搜索方向，该子问题基于目标函数的梯度和Hessian构建；2）设计不精确变体IHSDA，使用Lanczos过程近似求解子问题，保持单循环结构；3）算法在近正半定Hessian区域仍能保证下降方向具有充分负曲率。

Result: 理论证明：HSDA在$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-3/2})$迭代内找到$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon,\sqrt{\varepsilon})$-二阶稳定点，达到该问题类二阶方法的最优$\varepsilon$阶迭代复杂度。IHSDA以高概率达到相同迭代复杂度，总Hessian-向量乘积成本为$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-7/4})$。实验验证：在合成极小极大问题和对抗训练任务中表现出实际有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HSDA算法通过齐次特征值子问题在近正半定Hessian区域仍能保证充分负曲率下降方向，实现了非凸-强凹极小极大优化问题的最优二阶收敛速率。不精确变体IHSDA在保持理论保证的同时适用于大规模应用，为对抗训练等实际任务提供了高效稳定的优化工具。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel Homogeneous Second-order Descent Ascent (HSDA) algorithm for nonconvex-strongly concave minimax optimization problems. At each iteration, HSDA uniquely computes a search direction by solving a homogenized eigenvalue subproblem built from the gradient and Hessian of the objective function. This formulation guarantees a descent direction with sufficient negative curvature even in near-positive-semidefinite Hessian regimes--a key feature that enhances escape from saddle points. We prove that HSDA finds an $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon,\sqrt{\varepsilon})$-second-order stationary point within $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-3/2})$ iterations, matching the optimal $\varepsilon$-order iteration complexity among second-order methods for this problem class. To address large-scale applications, we further design an inexact variant (IHSDA) that preserves the single-loop structure while solving the subproblem approximately via a Lanczos procedure. With high probability, IHSDA achieves the same $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-3/2})$ iteration complexity and attains an $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon, \sqrt{\varepsilon})$-second-order stationary point, with the total Hessian-vector product cost bounded by $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-7/4})$. Experiments on synthetic minimax problems and adversarial training tasks confirm the practical effectiveness and robustness of the proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [161] [Subgradient Gliding Method for Nonsmooth Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14139)
*Zhihan Zhu,Yanhao Zhang,Yong Xia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文分析了经典投影次梯度方法在非光滑凸优化中的基本限制：边界点处缺乏有效次梯度会导致方法失效。作者提出了一种新的次梯度滑翔方法来解决这一问题，该方法在边界点处依然有效，并扩展了步长设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典投影次梯度方法在非光滑凸优化中存在一个根本性限制：当迭代点位于目标函数定义域的边界时，可能不存在有效的次梯度，导致算法在单次迭代后就以任意接近1的概率失败。这种限制严重影响了该方法在非Lipschitz非光滑凸问题中的应用范围。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的次梯度滑翔方法，该方法不需要边界点处的次梯度，避免了经典方法的过早终止问题。该方法将经典投影次梯度方法作为特例包含在内，并显著扩展了可容许的步长设计空间。方法还扩展到随机设置，且分析不依赖于目标函数的全局Lipschitz连续性，仅需对次梯度增长进行温和控制。

Result: 建立了最优遍历收敛率：凸问题为O(1/√t)，强凸问题为O(1/t)。数值实验表明，在经典投影次梯度方法完全失败的场景中，所提方法以100%的成功率可靠收敛，并在精度和收敛速度上实现了数量级的改进。

Conclusion: 次梯度滑翔方法解决了经典投影次梯度方法在边界点处缺乏有效次梯度的根本问题，显著扩展了次梯度基优化方法在非Lipschitz非光滑凸问题中的应用范围，提供了更大的算法设计灵活性。

Abstract: We identify and analyze a fundamental limitation of the classical projected subgradient method in nonsmooth convex optimization: the inevitable failure caused by the absence of valid subgradients at boundary points. We show that, under standard step sizes for both convex and strongly convex objectives, the method can fail after a single iteration with probability arbitrarily close to one, even on simple problem instances. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel alternative termed the \textit{subgradient gliding method}, which remains well defined without boundary subgradients and avoids premature termination. Beyond resolving this foundational issue, the proposed framework encompasses the classical projected subgradient method as a special case and substantially enlarges its admissible step-size design space, providing greater flexibility for algorithmic design. We establish optimal ergodic convergence rates, $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{t})$ for convex problems and $\mathcal{O}(1/t)$ for strongly convex problems, and further extend the framework to stochastic settings. Notably, our analysis does not rely on global Lipschitz continuity of the objective function, requiring only mild control on subgradient growth. Numerical experiments demonstrate that, in scenarios where the classical projected subgradient method fails completely, the proposed method converges reliably with a $100\%$ success rate and achieves orders-of-magnitude improvements in accuracy and convergence speed. These results substantially expand the scope of subgradient-based optimization methods to non-Lipschitz nonsmooth convex problems.

</details>


### [162] [Stable representations of Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equations with infinite horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14156)
*Arkadiusz Misztela,Sławomir Plaskacz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 构建无限时域状态约束下Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程的合适正则表示，利用Frankowska和Basco定理证明解的存在唯一性，并展示表示的稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限时域状态约束下的Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程，需要建立合适的数学框架来处理这类具有挑战性的控制问题，特别是存在状态约束时的解的存在性和唯一性问题

Method: 构造适当正则的表示形式，将问题转化为可应用Frankowska和Basco定理（2019）的形式，从而证明解的存在性和唯一性，并分析表示的稳定性

Result: 成功构建了无限时域状态约束HJB方程的正则表示，证明了在该表示下解的存在性和唯一性，并验证了表示的稳定性，通过具体示例进行了说明

Conclusion: 提出的正则表示方法为处理无限时域状态约束下的HJB方程提供了有效的数学工具，通过Frankowska和Basco定理建立了严格的解理论框架，并证明了方法的稳定性

Abstract: In this paper, for the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation with an infinite horizon and state constraints, we construct a suitably regular representation. This allows us to reduce the problem of existence and uniqueness of solutions to the Frankowska and Basco theorem from (2019). Furthermore, we demonstrate that our representations are stable. The obtained results are illustrated with examples.

</details>


### [163] [Smoothing Meets Perturbation: Unified and Tight Analysis for Nonconvex-Concave Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14185)
*Jiajin Li,Mahesh Nagarajan,Siyu Pan,Nanxi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了光滑非凸-凹极小极大优化问题，分析了扰动和平滑两种加速机制，建立了统一分析框架，设计了改进复杂度边界的新算法，并证明了匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然扰动和平滑两种加速机制都能改善收敛保证，但它们各自的作用和相对优势仍不清楚。需要建立统一的分析框架来分离和量化这两种机制的作用，从而设计更高效的算法。

Method: 开发了统一的分析框架来分离和量化平滑与扰动的作用。基于此框架设计了新的单循环和双循环一阶方法，改进了近似游戏平稳点(GS)和优化平稳点(OS)的迭代复杂度边界。同时构建了困难的实例来建立匹配的下界。

Result: 新算法在近似GS和OS的迭代复杂度边界上改进了现有最优结果。建立了匹配的下界，证明所得复杂度边界是紧的。揭示了近似GS和OS在内在复杂性行为上的根本差异。

Conclusion: 平滑和扰动在实现近似GS中扮演根本不同但互补的角色，它们的组合产生协同效应，比单独使用任一机制收敛更快。而仅靠扰动本身不足以实现OS。这些结果深化了对极小极大优化中加速机制的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate smooth nonconvex-concave minimax optimization problems and analyze two widely used acceleration mechanisms -- perturbation and smoothing. Perturbation augments the dual objective with a small quadratic regularization term, whereas smoothing employs an auxiliary primal sequence to approximate a proximal-point update of the value function. While both techniques are known to improve convergence guarantees, their respective roles and relative strengths remain unclear. We develop a unified analytical framework that disentangles and quantifies the respective roles of smoothing and perturbation. With this analytical framework, we design new first-order methods that improve the state-of-the-art iteration complexity bounds for both single-loop and double-loop schemes, for achieving both approximate game stationary (GS) and optimization stationary (OS) points. We also establish matching lower bounds based on carefully constructed hard instances, showing that the resulting complexity bounds are tight. Taken together, these results reveal a fundamental difference between approximate GS and OS in terms of their intrinsic complexity behavior and the following understanding: smoothing and perturbation play fundamentally different yet complementary roles in achieving approximate GS. Their combination creates a synergistic effect that yields strictly faster convergence speed than either mechanism alone, whereas perturbation by itself is insufficient for OS.

</details>


### [164] [Graph-Guided Fused Regularization for Single- and Multi-Task Regression on Spatiotemporal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14480)
*Meixia Lin,Ziyang Zeng,Yangjing Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种用于时空矩阵回归的正则化框架，通过融合惩罚和图引导惩罚分别建模时间平滑性和空间相似性，并扩展到多任务设置，具有理论保证和高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空矩阵数据在现代应用中频繁出现，但由于复杂的维度特定依赖关系，进行有效的回归分析仍然具有挑战性。现有方法难以同时处理时间平滑演化和空间相似性模式。

Method: 提出正则化框架，包含融合惩罚捕捉时间平滑演化，图引导惩罚促进空间相似性。扩展到多任务设置实现跨相关任务的联合估计。开发基于Halpern Peaceman-Rachford方法的高效求解器。

Result: 理论证明提出估计量的统计一致性。算法实现快速全局非遍历O(1/k)收敛率且每次迭代复杂度低。数值实验显示在预测精度、估计误差、计算效率和可扩展性方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为时空矩阵回归提供了一种有效且可扩展的解决方案，能够同时建模时间和空间依赖关系，具有理论保证和计算效率，在多任务设置中表现优异。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal matrix-valued data arise frequently in modern applications, yet performing effective regression analysis remains challenging due to complex, dimension-specific dependencies. In this work, we propose a regularized framework for spatiotemporal matrix regression that characterizes temporal and spatial dependencies through tailored penalties. Specifically, the model incorporates a fused penalty to capture smooth temporal evolution and a graph-guided penalty to promote spatial similarity. The framework also extends to the multi-task setting, enabling joint estimation across related tasks. We provide a comprehensive analysis of the framework from both theoretical and computational perspectives. Theoretically, we establish the statistical consistency of the proposed estimators. Computationally, we develop an efficient solver based on the Halpern Peaceman-Rachford method for the resulting composite convex optimization problem. The proposed algorithm achieves a fast global non-ergodic $\mathcal{O}(1/k)$ convergence rate with low per-iteration complexity. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in terms of predictive accuracy and estimation error, while also exhibiting superior computational efficiency and scalability.

</details>


### [165] [On the Existence of Koopman Linear Embeddings for Controlled Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14537)
*Xu Shang,Masih Haseli,Jorge Cortés,Yang Zheng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了受控非线性系统存在精确有限维Koopman线性表示（Koopman线性嵌入）的充要条件，并引入了符号化程序来判断给定系统是否满足这些条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman线性表示已成为非线性系统控制设计的流行工具，但尚不清楚何时这种表示是精确的。本文旨在明确受控非线性系统存在精确有限维Koopman线性表示的条件。

Method: 首先证明此类系统必须可转换为特殊的控制仿射保持（CAP）结构，该结构强制状态对控制输入的仿射依赖并将所有非线性隔离到自治子系统中。然后证明该自治子系统本身必须具有足够丰富Koopman不变子空间的有限维Koopman线性模型。最后引入符号化程序来判断给定受控非线性系统是否满足CAP结构。

Result: 建立了受控非线性系统存在精确有限维Koopman线性表示的充要条件：系统必须可转换为CAP结构，且自治子系统必须具有足够丰富Koopman不变子空间的有限维Koopman线性模型。符号化程序能够区分Koopman近似误差是源于系统内在动力学还是提升函数的选择。

Conclusion: 本文为Koopman线性表示在非线性控制中的精确性提供了理论框架，明确了精确表示存在的条件，并提供了判断给定系统是否满足这些条件的实用方法，有助于理解Koopman近似误差的来源。

Abstract: Koopman linear representations have become a popular tool for control design of nonlinear systems, yet it remains unclear when such representations are exact. In this paper, we establish sufficient and necessary conditions under which a controlled nonlinear system admits an exact finite-dimensional Koopman linear representation, which we term Koopman linear embedding. We show that such a system must be transformable into a special control-affine preserved (CAP) structure, which enforces affine dependence of the state on the control input and isolates all nonlinearities into an autonomous subsystem. We further prove that this autonomous subsystem must itself admit a finite-dimensional Koopman linear model with a sufficiently-rich Koopman invariant subspace. Finally, we introduce a symbolic procedure to determine whether a given controlled nonlinear system admits the CAP structure, thereby elucidating whether Koopman approximation errors arise from intrinsic system dynamics or from the choice of lifting functions.

</details>


### [166] [Extragradient methods for mean field games of controls and mean field type FBSDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14621)
*Charles Meynard*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种求解由单调向量场驱动的耦合平均场前向-后向随机微分方程的数值方案，基于外梯度方法，将解表征为希尔伯特空间中单调变分不等式的零点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决由单调向量场驱动的耦合平均场前向-后向随机微分方程的数值求解问题，这类方程在平均场博弈和控制理论中具有重要意义，但缺乏有效的数值方法。

Method: 采用外梯度方法，将解表征为希尔伯特空间中单调变分不等式的零点。首先在平均场控制博弈背景下引入该方法，并揭示其与虚拟博弈的联系。在足够强的单调性假设下，证明近似解序列以指数速度收敛。

Result: 在足够强的单调性假设下，证明了近似解序列以指数速度收敛。然后将该方法扩展到不一定源于最优控制的一般前向-后向随机微分方程系统。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于外梯度方法的数值方案，能够有效求解由单调向量场驱动的耦合平均场前向-后向随机微分方程，并在单调性条件下保证指数收敛性，该方法可推广到更一般的随机微分方程系统。

Abstract: In this paper we present a numerical scheme to solve coupled mean field forward-backward stochastic differential equations driven by monotone vector fields. This is based on an adaptation of so called extragradient methods by characterizing solutions as zeros of monotone variational inequalities in a Hilbert space. We first introduce the procedure in the context of mean field games of controls and highlight its connection to the fictitious play. Under sufficiently strong monotonicity assumptions, we demonstrate that the sequence of approximate solutions converges exponentially fast. Then we extend the method and main results to general forward backward systems of stochastic differential equations that do not necessarily stem from optimal control.

</details>


### [167] [Interwoven SDP in Primal-Dual Proximal Splitting Methods for Adjustable Robust Convex Optimisation with SOS-Convex Polynomial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14624)
*Neil D. Dizon,Bethany I. Caldwell,Vaithilingam Jeyakumar,Guoyin Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种求解两阶段可调鲁棒凸优化问题的新方法，该方法适用于具有一般凸目标函数和平方和凸多项式约束的问题，通过二次决策规则和原始-对偶近端分裂算法实现高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段可调鲁棒凸优化问题在决策应用中广泛存在，但由于包含平方和凸多项式约束，难以转化为可直接求解的凸优化模型（如锥线性规划），现有软件无法直接处理这类问题。

Method: 1. 证明在二次决策规则下，鲁棒问题可等价表示为凸复合无约束优化模型；2. 开发定制的原始-对偶近端分裂方法；3. 利用半定规划技术、凸分析和实代数几何工具，建立理论性质，包括基于SDP的闭凸集投影计算公式。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法在具有线性及平方和凸多项式存储成本的两阶段批量生产模型（需求不确定）中表现出有效性和适用性，能够将SDP技术融入原始-对偶近端分裂框架。

Conclusion: 该方法扩展了原始-对偶近端分裂方法可有效应用的问题类别，为求解具有平方和凸多项式约束的两阶段鲁棒优化问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a novel methodology for solving a two-stage adjustable robust convex optimisation problem with a general (proximable) convex objective function and constraints defined by sum-of-squares (SOS) convex polynomials. These problems appear in many decision-making applications. However, they are challenging to solve and typically cannot be reformulated as numerically tractable convex optimisation models, such as conic linear programs, that can be solved directly using existing software. We show that the robust problem admits an equivalent representation as a convex composite unconstrained optimisation model that preserves the same objective values, under quadratic decision rules on the adjustable decision variables. Building on this reformulation, we develop a tailored first-order primal-dual proximal splitting method. By leveraging semidefinite programming (SDP) techniques as well as tools from convex analysis and real algebraic geometry, we establish its theoretical properties, including computable SDP-based formulas for projections onto closed convex sets, specified by SOS-convex polynomial inequalities. Numerical experiments on a two-stage lot-sizing model with both linear as well as SOS-convex polynomial storage costs under demand uncertainty demonstrate the effectiveness and applicability of the proposed approach. Our approach enables the incorporation of SDP techniques into a primal-dual proximal splitting framework, thereby broadening the class of problems to which these methods can be effectively applied.

</details>


### [168] [Second-order conditions for bang-bang control of elliptic equations in arbitrary dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14632)
*Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究具有bang-bang型最优控制的半线性PDE最优控制问题，利用Bessel势空间理论，通过二阶最优性条件刻画目标函数的二次增长特性，且该方法适用于任意空间维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对bang-bang型最优控制的半线性PDE最优控制问题，现有方法在空间维度上存在限制，需要发展适用于任意维度的二阶最优性条件分析框架。

Method: 利用Bessel势空间理论，通过二阶最优性条件分析目标函数的二次增长特性，该方法突破了空间维度的限制，适用于任意维度的情形。

Result: 建立了适用于任意空间维度的bang-bang型最优控制二阶最优性条件分析框架，成功刻画了目标函数的二次增长特性。

Conclusion: 通过Bessel势空间理论，本文发展了适用于任意空间维度的bang-bang型最优控制二阶最优性条件分析方法，为相关最优控制问题的理论研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We consider an optimal control problem governed by a semilinear PDE in cases where the optimal control is of bang-bang type. By utilizing the theory of Bessel potential space, we characterize quadratic growth of the objective via a second-order optimality condition. In contrast to previous contributions, our method of proof works in arbitrary spatial dimensions.

</details>


### [169] [Distributed Multi-Step Model Predictive Control for Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14714)
*Navid Noroozi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种分布式多步模型预测控制框架，用于解决时变有向通信、状态和输入约束下的离散时间多智能体系统一致性问题，通过引入字典序平局打破机制确保严格相对内部性以实现渐近一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解决多智能体系统在时变有向通信、状态和输入约束下的一致性问题。现有方法中，强制终端状态包含在邻居凸包内可以保证凸包不变性，但通常无法保证收敛所需的严格相对内部性，这可能导致系统无法达到渐近一致。

Method: 采用基于凸包不变性和严格内部性的几何视角，将一致性问题重新表述为协议集的稳定化问题。提出分布式多步模型预测控制框架，引入字典序平局打破机制：在最优（或接近最优）MPC解中，通过次要准则选择相对于邻居凸包最大化内部性度量的轨迹。推导了单积分器和双积分器智能体在输入有界情况下的显式时域条件。

Result: 证明了当存在内部可行终端状态时，所提出的选择规则能够强制执行渐近一致所需的严格性条件。通过数值模拟展示了单调直径衰减特性，并报告了每个智能体的计算复杂度。为单积分器和双积分器智能体推导了确保可行性和内部可行终端点自动存在的显式时域条件。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种通过有限步集合-Lyapunov收缩实现一致的分布式可实施方案。通过引入字典序平局打破机制，在不缩小可行集或改变主要性能目标的情况下解决了严格相对内部性问题，确保了多智能体系统在约束条件下的渐近一致。

Abstract: This paper studies consensus of discrete-time multi-agent systems under time-varying directed communication, state and input constraints using a distributed multi-step model predictive control (MPC) framework. Consensus is recast as stabilization of the agreement set, and a geometric viewpoint based on convex-hull invariance and strict interiority is adopted. Building on an existing geometric necessary and sufficient condition for agreement, we show that enforcing terminal inclusion in local neighbor convex hulls guarantees hull invariance but does not, in general, imply the strict relative-interior property required for convergence. An explicit counterexample demonstrates that strictness cannot be deduced from feasibility and contraction constraints alone.
  To resolve this issue without shrinking feasible sets or altering primary performance objectives, a lexicographic tie-breaking mechanism is introduced. Among optimal (or near-optimal) MPC solutions, the proposed secondary criterion selects trajectories maximizing an interiority measure with respect to the neighbor hull. It is shown that whenever an interior feasible terminal state exists, this selection rule enforces the strictness condition required for asymptotic consensus. Explicit horizon conditions are derived for single- and double-integrator agents with bounded inputs, ensuring feasibility and automatic existence of interior feasible terminal points. The resulting scheme provides a distributed and implementable route to consensus via finite-step set-Lyapunov contraction. Numerical simulations with distributed inter-process communication illustrate monotone diameter decay and report per-agent computational complexity.

</details>


### [170] [Reciprocal Specific Relative Entropy between Continuous Martingales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14776)
*Julio Backhoff,Xin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出连续鞅间的新散度度量——倒数特定相对熵，证明其在赢鞅集合上的最小化问题解为著名的中性Wright-Fisher扩散过程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多个角度提出连续鞅间散度的新定义需求，特别是在预测市场模型（赢鞅）的背景下，需要一种合适的散度度量来识别"最显著"的赢鞅

Method: 引入倒数特定相对熵作为连续鞅间的新散度定义，研究其在赢鞅集合上的最小化问题，并通过扰动退化的鞅最优输运问题（方差最小化）来验证结果的合理性

Result: 证明倒数特定相对熵最小化问题的优化器是著名的中性Wright-Fisher扩散过程，该过程在某种意义上是最显著的赢鞅，且在适当扰动退化问题时被唯一选择

Conclusion: 倒数特定相对熵为连续鞅提供了有意义的散度度量，中性Wright-Fisher扩散过程作为其最小化问题的解，在预测市场模型中具有特殊地位，是"最自然"的赢鞅选择

Abstract: We introduce a novel notion of divergence between continuous martingales; the reciprocal specific relative entropy. First, we motivate this definition from multiple perspectives. Thereafter, we solve the reciprocal specific relative entropy minimization problem over the set of win-martingales (used as models for prediction markets Aldous (2013)). Surprisingly, we show that the optimizer is the renowned neutral Wright-Fisher diffusion. We also justify that this diffusion is in a sense the most salient win-martingale, since it is uniquely selected when we suitably perturb the degenerate martingale optimal transport problem of variance minimization.

</details>


### [171] [Effective approximations of solutions to highly oscillatory diffusion equations from coarse measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14820)
*Claude Le Bris,Frédéric Legoll,Simon Ruget*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 基于部分信息（全局能量）从振荡系数扩散方程重构有效常数系数的非凸优化方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅能获取部分信息（全局能量）的情况下，解决从高度振荡系数的扩散方程重构有效常数系数的问题。虽然微观结构重构是病态问题，但证明有效系数重构是可行的。

Method: 提出非凸优化策略，利用均质化理论作为严格基础。通过部分信息（全局能量）重构有效常数系数，并详细讨论算法实现。

Result: 提供全面的数值算例，证明该策略的实用价值。改进了先前工作[C. Le Bris, F. Legoll and S. Lemaire, COCV 2018; C. Le Bris, F. Legoll and K. Li, CRAS 2013]。

Conclusion: 即使仅拥有粗粒度信息，重构有效系数是可行的。该方法为基于部分信息的有效参数识别提供了理论框架和实用工具。

Abstract: We approximate a diffusion equation with highly oscillatory coefficients with a diffusion equation with constant coefficients. The approach is put in action in contexts where only partial information (namely the global energy stored in the physical system) is available. While the reconstruction of the microstructure is known to be an ill-posed problem, we show that the reconstruction of effective coefficients is possible and this even with only some coarse information. The strategy we present takes the form of a non-convex optimization problem. Homogenization theory provides elements for a rigorous foundation of the approach. Some algorithmic aspects are discussed in details. We provide a comprehensive set of numerical illustrations that demonstrate the practical interest of our strategy. The present work improves on the earlier works [C. Le Bris, F. Legoll and S. Lemaire, COCV 2018; C. Le Bris, F. Legoll and K. Li, CRAS 2013].

</details>


### [172] [On Convergence Analysis of Network-GIANT: An approximate Hessian-based fully distributed optimization algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14830)
*Souvik Das,Luca Schenato,Subhrakanti Dey*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: Network-GIANT分布式优化方法的收敛性分析：该近似牛顿型方法在保持与一阶方法相同通信复杂度的同时，实现了更快的线性收敛速度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Network-GIANT方法在经验上显示出比一阶分布式方法更快的线性收敛特性，但缺乏理论收敛性分析。本文旨在为其提供严格的收敛性证明和收敛率量化分析

Method: 基于共识的参数更新，结合局部Hessian下降方向和梯度跟踪，推导全局线性收敛率。收敛率表示为3×3矩阵的谱半径，该矩阵依赖于目标函数的Lipschitz连续性(L)、强凸性(μ)参数以及底层无向图的谱范数(σ)

Result: 给出了步长参数η的显式上界，保证谱半径小于1。推导了最优性间隙范数的混合线性-二次不等式上界，证明当算法接近全局最优时，在步长较小且Hessian近似误差γ<μ的条件下，可实现局部线性收敛率1-η(1-γ/μ)

Conclusion: Network-GIANT的渐近线性收敛率约为1-η，这首次解释了其更快的收敛速度。数值实验使用CovType数据集进行二元逻辑回归验证了理论结果

Abstract: In this paper, we present a detailed convergence analysis of a recently developed approximate Newton-type fully distributed optimization method for smooth, strongly convex local loss functions, called Network-GIANT, which has been empirically illustrated to show faster linear convergence properties while having the same communication complexity (per iteration) as its first order distributed counterparts. By using consensus based parameter updates, and a local Hessian based descent direction at the individual nodes with gradient tracking, we first explicitly characterize a global linear convergence rate for Network-GIANT, which can be computed as the spectral radius of a $3 \times 3$ matrix dependent on the Lipschitz continuity ($L$) and strong convexity ($μ$) parameters of the objective functions, and the spectral norm ($σ$) of the underlying undirected graph represented by a doubly stochastic consensus matrix. We provide an explicit bound on the step size parameter $η$, below which this spectral radius is guaranteed to be less than $1$. Furthermore, we derive a mixed linear-quadratic inequality based upper bound for the optimality gap norm, which allows us to conclude that, under small step size values, asymptotically, as the algorithm approaches the global optimum, it achieves a locally linear convergence rate of $1-η(1 -\fracγμ)$ for Network-GIANT, provided the Hessian approximation error $γ$ (between the harmonic mean of the local Hessians and the global hessian (the arithmetic mean of the local Hessians) is smaller than $μ$. This asymptotically linear convergence rate of $\approx 1-η$ explains the faster convergence rate of Network-GIANT for the first time. Numerical experiments are carried out with a reduced CovType dataset for binary logistic regression over a variety of graphs to illustrate the above theoretical results.

</details>


### [173] [Pattern preservation in finite to infinite-horizon optimal control problems for dissipative systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14944)
*Matteo Della Rossa,Thiago Alves Lima,Lorenzo Freddi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了耗散系统无限时域最优控制问题与有限时域公式之间的关系，证明了耗散性（存在强制存储函数）可保证无限时域最优控制可通过有限时域最优控制的极限获得，这一性质称为模式保持性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究耗散系统的无限时域最优控制问题与有限时域公式之间的关系，旨在建立耗散性理论与最优控制中变分收敛框架的形式联系，为验证模式保持性提供具体且数值可处理的充分条件。

Method: 通过分析耗散系统的状态方程（存在强制存储函数），建立耗散性与模式保持性之间的理论联系，利用变分收敛框架证明无限时域最优控制可通过有限时域最优控制的极限获得。

Result: 证明了对于一大类问题，状态方程的耗散性（当存在强制存储函数时）意味着无限时域最优控制可以通过相应有限时域最优控制的极限获得，即模式保持性成立。建立了耗散性理论与变分收敛框架的形式联系。

Conclusion: 耗散性为验证模式保持性提供了具体且数值可处理的充分条件，数值示例展示了所提充分条件的有效性和局限性，建立了耗散系统最优控制中无限时域与有限时域问题之间的理论桥梁。

Abstract: This paper focuses on infinite-horizon optimal control problems for dissipative systems and the relations to their finite-horizon formulations. We show that, for a large class of problems, dissipativity of the state equation, when a coercive storage function exists, implies that infinite-horizon optimal controls can be obtained as limits of the corresponding finite-horizon ones. This property is referred to as pattern preservation, or pattern-preserving property.
  Our analysis establishes a formal link between dissipativity theory and the variational convergence framework in optimal control, thus providing a concrete and numerically tractable condition for verifying pattern preservation. Numerical examples illustrate the effectiveness and limitations of the proposed sufficient conditions.

</details>


### [174] [Max-Min Bilinear Completely Positive Programs: A Semidefinite Relaxation with Tightness Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14949)
*Sarah Yini Gao,Xindong Tang,Yancheng Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种处理完全正锥上最大-最小双线性优化问题的统一框架，通过COP-CP锥的线性重构，并开发了基于矩和平方和的半定松弛层次来求解这类NP难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大-最小双线性优化模型作为优化理论中的典型鞍点公式，广泛应用于二人零和博弈、鲁棒优化、分布鲁棒优化和对抗机器学习等领域。然而，当变量位于完全正锥时，这类问题具有NP难的计算复杂性，需要有效的求解方法。

Method: 1. 将完全正锥上的最大-最小双线性问题等价重构为COP-CP锥上的单阶段线性规划；2. 基于COP和CP锥的矩和平方和表示，开发半定松弛层次；3. 应用平截断条件验证松弛紧致性；4. 在温和条件下保证层次紧致性。

Result: 1. 建立了COP-CP锥上的线性重构等价性；2. 开发了半定松弛层次，在温和条件下保证紧致性；3. 框架扩展了现有分布鲁棒优化和多项式博弈的CP/COP方法；4. 在循环Colonel Blotto博弈的多个实例中，半定松弛满足平截断条件，精确求解了混合策略均衡。

Conclusion: 该研究为完全正锥上的最大-最小双线性优化问题提供了一个统一的求解框架，通过COP-CP锥重构和半定松弛层次，有效处理了这类NP难问题，并在经典博弈实例中验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Max-min bilinear optimization models, where one agent maximizes and an adversary minimizes a common bilinear objective, serve as canonical saddle-point formulations in optimization theory. They capture, among others, two-player zero-sum games, robust and distributionally robust optimization, and adversarial machine learning. This study focuses on the subclass whose variables lie in the completely positive (CP) cone, capturing a broad family of mixed-binary quadratic max-min problems through the modelling power of completely positive programming. We show that such problems admit an equivalent single-stage linear reformulation over the COP-CP cone, defined as the Cartesian product of the copositive (COP) and CP cones. Because testing membership in COP cones is co-NP-complete, the resulting COP-CP program inherits NP-hardness. To address this challenge, we develop a hierarchy of semidefinite relaxations based on moment and sum-of-squares representations of the COP and CP cones, and flat truncation conditions are applied to certify the tightness. We show that the tightness of the hierarchy is guaranteed under mild conditions. The framework extends existing CP/COP approaches for distributionally robust optimization and polynomial games. We apply the framework to the cyclic Colonel Blotto game, an extension of Borel's classic allocation contest. Across multiple instances, the semidefinite relaxation meets the flat-truncation conditions and solves the exact mixed-strategy equilibrium.

</details>


### [175] [ALiA: Adaptive Linearized ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15000)
*Uijeong Jang,Kaizhao Sun,Wotao Yin,Ernest K Ryu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: ALiA是一种自适应ADMM变体，基于函数线性化近端ADMM，通过自适应步长选择提升收敛速度，无需回溯线搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ADMM及其变体在步长选择上通常需要回溯线搜索，计算成本较高。受自适应梯度和近端方法启发，需要开发无需回溯的自适应ADMM方法，提升收敛效率。

Method: ALiA基于函数线性化近端ADMM框架，引入自适应步长选择机制，无需回溯线搜索。进一步开发了替代步长选择方案，以可忽略的计算开销提升收敛速度。

Result: 理论证明了ALiA在凸可微目标函数下的点收敛性。数值实验显示ALiA比标准FLiP ADMM收敛更快，在适用问题类别中优于或匹配现有自适应方法的实际性能。

Conclusion: ALiA是一种高效的自适应ADMM变体，通过自适应步长选择机制实现了无需回溯线搜索的快速收敛，在理论和实验上均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: We propose ALiA, a novel adaptive variant of the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM). Specifically, ALiA is a variant of function-linearized proximal ADMM (FLiP ADMM), which generalizes the classical ADMM by leveraging the differentiable structure of the objective function, making it highly versatile. Notably, ALiA features an adaptive stepsize selection scheme that eliminates the need for backtracking linesearch. Motivated by recent advances in adaptive gradient and proximal methods, we establish point convergence of ALiA for convex and differentiable objectives. Furthermore, by introducing negligible computational overhead, we develop an alternative stepsize selection scheme for ALiA that improves the convergence speed both theoretically and empirically. Extensive numerical experiments on practical datasets confirm the accelerated performance of ALiA compared to standard FLiP ADMM. Additionally, we demonstrate that ALiA either outperforms or matches the practical performance of existing adaptive methods across problem classes where it is applicable.

</details>

Wessel,E. Won,X. P. Xu,B. D. Yabsley,W. Yan,W. Yan,J. Yelton,K. Yi,J. H. Yin,K. Yoshihara,C. Z. Yuan,J. Yuan,L. Zani,F. Zeng,B. Zhang,V. Zhilich,J. S. Zhou,Q. D. Zhou,L. Zhu,R. Žlebčík*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: Belle II实验利用427.9 fb⁻¹数据，通过初态辐射方法测量了e⁺e⁻ → h⁺h⁻J/ψ (h=π/K/p)在3.8 GeV至5.5/6.0/7.0 GeV能区的截面，首次研究了p̄pJ/ψ过程，并进行了矢量粲偶素态和中间态的寻找。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究e⁺e⁻ → h⁺h⁻J/ψ过程对于理解粲偶素物理、寻找新的矢量粲偶素态以及探索强子产生机制具有重要意义。特别是p̄pJ/ψ过程此前从未被研究过，可能揭示新的物理现象。

Method: 使用Belle II探测器在Υ(4S)和Υ(10753)共振峰附近收集的427.9 fb⁻¹数据样本，通过初态辐射方法测量截面。对π⁺π⁻J/ψ和K⁺K⁻J/ψ过程与先前Belle结果进行联合分析，首次研究p̄pJ/ψ过程，并在h⁺h⁻J/ψ和h⁺J/ψ系统中寻找可能的矢量粲偶素态和中间态。

Result: π⁺π⁻J/ψ和K⁺K⁻J/ψ截面与先前发表结果一致；p̄pJ/ψ过程产额较小，截面随能量变化未发现显著结构；在h⁺h⁻J/ψ系统中未发现明显的矢量粲偶素态信号，在h⁺J/ψ系统中也未发现显著的中间态证据。

Conclusion: Belle II实验提供了e⁺e⁻ → h⁺h⁻J/ψ过程的精确截面测量，验证了先前结果，首次探索了p̄pJ/ψ过程但未发现新物理迹象。这些结果为粲偶素物理和强子产生机制研究提供了重要约束。

Abstract: Using a data sample of 427.9 fb$^{-1}$ collected by the Belle~II detector at or near the $Υ(4S)$ and $Υ(10753)$ resonances, the cross sections for $e^+e^-\to h^+h^-J/ψ$ $(h=π/K/p)$ at center-of-mass energies ranging from 3.8 GeV or the production threshold to 5.5/6.0/7.0 GeV have been measured via initial-state radiation. The cross sections for the processes $e^+e^-\to π^+π^-J/ψ$ and $e^+e^-\to K^+K^-J/ψ$ are consistent with previously published results. The cross sections for these channels obtained by combining with previous Belle results are also given. The process $e^+e^-\to p\bar p J/ψ$ is investigated for the first time. The yields are small and no significant structure is observed in the cross section versus energy. Searches for vector charmonium-like states in the $h^+h^-J/ψ$ systems, and for associated intermediate states in the $h^{\pm} J/ψ$ systems, are also presented.

</details>

Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LHCb实验升级后的数据采集系统在40MHz束流交叉频率下实时重建VELO像素探测器中的所有击中点，并利用这些实时重建的团簇开发了新的亮度测量和发光区域监测方法，该方法已在2024年物理运行中投入使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用升级后LHCb实验数据采集系统中实时重建的VELO像素探测器团簇，开发一种直接在探测器读出级别进行亮度测量和发光区域监测的新方法，以替代或补充传统的亮度测量技术。

Method: 在VELO读出FPGA中实现实时团簇计数器阵列，利用40MHz束流交叉频率下实时重建的VELO像素探测器团簇进行实时分析。该方法具有优于100ms的本征时间粒度，为分析目的降采样至3秒。

Result: 该方法在质子-质子和铅-铅碰撞数据上均表现出优异性能：统计分辨率优于百分之一水平，对可变运行条件的敏感性达到相同水平，在包含LHCb Run 3工作点的亮度范围内非线性行为与零兼容。

Conclusion: 基于VELO实时团簇重建的亮度测量方法在LHCb实验中成功实现并运行，提供了高精度、实时的亮度监测能力，为实验运行和数据分析提供了重要工具。

Abstract: The data acquisition system of the upgraded LHCb experiment includes the fast reconstruction of all hits in the vertex locator (VELO) pixel detector at the beam-crossing rate of 40 MHz, implemented as on-the-fly clustering embedded in the firmware of the readout board FPGAs. The availability of a high rate of reconstructed clusters in real time enables a new fast approach for measuring luminosity and monitoring the LHCb luminous region, directly at the detector readout level. This methodology has been implemented as an array of real-time cluster counters in the VELO readout FPGAs and has been in operation since the start of the 2024 physics run of LHCb. This paper describes the methodology and its features and performance, both on proton-proton and lead-lead collision data. The method shows a statistical resolution better than the percent level, and a sensitivity to variable running conditions of the same level. This is achieved with an intrinsic time granularity better than 100 ms , undersampled to 3 s for analysis purposes. Nonlinear behaviour is compatible with zero in a luminosity range including the LHCb Run 3 operating point.

</details>


### [178] [Characterization of argon recoils at the keV scale with ReD and ReD+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14538)
*L. Pandola,P. Agnes,I. Ahmad,S. Albergo,I. Albuquerque,M. Atzori Corona,M. Ave,B. Bottino,M. Cadeddu,A. Caminata,N. Canci,M. Caravati,L. Consiglio,S. Davini,M. De Napoli,L. K. S. Dias,G. Dolganov,G. Fiorillo,D. Franco,M. Gulino,T. Hessel,N. Kemmerich,M. Kimura,M. Kuźniak,M. La Commara,J. Machts,G. Matteucci,E. Moura Santos,E. Nikoloudaki,V. Oleynikov,R. Perez Varona,N. Pino,S. M. R. Puglia,M. Rescigno,D. Sablone,B. Sales Costa,S. Sanfilippo,C. Sunny,Y. Suvorov,R. Tartaglia,G. Testera,A. Tricomi,M. Wada,Y. Wang,R. Wojaczyński,P. Zakhary*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ReD实验使用双相时间投影室测量了2-10 keV范围内氩核反冲的电离产额Qy，发现低能区Qy更高，填补了7 keV以下空白数据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为氩基暗物质探测器提供低能核反冲电离产额的精确测量数据，特别是针对低质量弱相互作用大质量粒子(WIMP)探测，该能量范围的探测器响应建模对探测灵敏度至关重要

Method: 使用Cf-252裂变源产生的中子辐照双相时间投影室，测量氩核反冲在2-10 keV能量范围内的电离产额Qy

Result: 实验填补了7 keV以下能量范围的测量空白，确认了7 keV以上数据与先前结果一致，并发现低能区电离产额Qy更高

Conclusion: 该测量为氩基暗物质实验提供了关键的低能核反冲响应数据，对低质量WIMP探测的灵敏度和探测器建模具有重要意义

Abstract: The ReD experiment measured the ionization yield Qy of argon for nuclear recoils in the 2-10 keV range using a dual-phase Time Projection Chamber irradiated with neutrons from a Cf-252 fission source. The measurement extends coverage below 7 keV, confirms consistency with previous data above 7 keV, and indicates a higher Qy at lower energies. These results are relevant for argon-based experiments searching for dark matter in the form of low-mass Weakly Interacting Massive Particles, which are very sensitive to the modeling of the detector response in this energy range.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [$H_0(C_c(\mathcal{G}_\bullet,\mathbb{Z}))\neq H_0^{\mathrm{sing}}(B\mathcal{G};\mathbb{Z})$ for the Cantor unit groupoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13375)
*Luciano Melodia*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明对于ample群胚，Matui型群胚同调与分类空间的奇异同调不一定相等，并以康托集上的单位群胚为例展示这种差异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究ample群胚的Matui型群胚同调与分类空间奇异同调之间的关系，验证两者是否一致

Method: 通过神经复形构造Moore链复形，利用面映射的推前映射计算Matui型群胚同调，并与分类空间的奇异同调进行比较

Result: 发现Matui型群胚同调与分类空间奇异同调不一定相等，差异在0维同调就已出现，以康托集上的单位群胚为例证明了这种差异

Conclusion: 对于ample群胚，Matui型群胚同调与分类空间的奇异同调是不同的同调理论，两者在一般情况下不相等

Abstract: For an ample groupoid $\mathcal{G}$, Matui type groupoid homology is computed from the nerve $\mathcal{G}_\bullet$ via Moore chains $C_c(\mathcal{G}_n,\mathbb{Z})$ and the alternating sum of pushforwards along the face maps. We give an explicit example showing that this homology need not agree with singular homology of the classifying space $B\mathcal{G}$. The discrepancy occurs already in degree $0$ for the unit groupoid on the Cantor set.

</details>


### [180] [Projective Constant Decompositions of Persistence Modules over Noetherian Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13560)
*Nadiya Upegui Keagy*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文将持久化模的结构与分解理论从域上的表示推广到诺特环上的酉左模，引入了射影常数型模的概念，并建立了在A_n型箭图、全序集和Zigzag偏序集上点态有限生成持久化模存在射影常数分解的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化模作为拓扑数据分析的代数基础，传统上研究域上偏序集的表示。本文旨在将这一理论推广到更一般的诺特环上的酉左模，以扩展持久化模的结构和分解理论的应用范围。

Method: 引入射影常数型模作为区间模的推广，研究其在A_n型箭图、全序集和Zigzag偏序集上的分解性质。通过建立射影余极限条件(PCC)和内部态射余核的射影性等代数判据，刻画点态有限生成持久化模存在射影常数分解的充要条件。

Result: 1. 对于箭图和Zigzag索引，射影常数分解存在当且仅当满足射影余极限条件(PCC)；2. 对于全序索引，射影常数分解存在当且仅当内部态射的余核是射影模；3. 在诺特环（特别是主理想整环）上，当有限生成射影模自由时，可得到经典的区间分解；4. 在Krull-Schmidt框架下证明了不可分解射影常数分解的唯一性，并将区间分解的唯一性推广到整环上的持久化模。

Conclusion: 本文成功将持久化模的分解理论从域推广到诺特环，建立了射影常数分解的完整理论框架，为拓扑数据分析提供了更广泛的代数基础，特别是在主理想整环等环上的应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Persistence modules serve as the algebraic foundation for topological data analysis, typically studied as representations of posets over a field. This article extends the structural and decomposition theory of persistence modules to the more general setting of unitary left modules over Noetherian rings. We introduce the notion of a module of projective constant type, a generalization of interval modules that facilitates decomposition results. We characterize the existence of projective constant decompositions for pointwise finitely generated persistence modules indexed by $A_n$-type quivers, totally ordered sets, and zigzag posets. Our primary results establish that such decompositions exist if and only if specific algebraic criteria are met: the projective colimit conditions (PCC) for quiver and zigzag indexings, and the projectivity of cokernels of internal morphisms for totally ordered indexings. Furthermore, we provide corollaries for the existence of classical interval decompositions over Noetherian rings where finitely generated projective modules are free, such as principal ideal domains (PIDs). Finally, we establish the uniqueness of indecomposable projective constant decompositions within the Krull-Schmidt framework and extend the uniqueness of interval decompositions to persistence modules over integral domains.

</details>


### [181] [m-Contigutiy Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14680)
*Nilay Ekiz Yazici,Nursultan Kuanyshov,Ayse Borat*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 论文系统发展了单纯形映射的m-邻接距离作为单纯复形范畴中同伦复杂度的离散逼近框架，构建了从下方逼近邻接距离的递增不变量序列，并应用于定义m-单纯形Lusternik-Schnirelmann范畴和m-离散拓扑复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为单纯复形范畴中的同伦复杂度问题提供离散逼近框架，通过m-邻接距离来量化单纯形映射之间的"距离"，从而建立系统的离散同伦理论工具。

Method: 系统发展m-邻接距离理论，构建递增不变量序列从下方逼近邻接距离，研究其基本性质（重分不变性、复合行为、范畴积不等式），并将理论应用于定义两个新的离散不变量。

Result: 建立了m-邻接距离的完整理论框架，证明了其在重心细分下的不变性、复合映射的行为规律以及范畴积不等式，成功定义了m-单纯形Lusternik-Schnirelmann范畴和m-离散拓扑复杂度。

Conclusion: m-邻接距离为单纯复形范畴中的同伦复杂度提供了有效的离散逼近框架，新定义的两个不变量是该理论的自然应用，为离散拓扑和计算拓扑研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we systematically develop the m-contiguity distance between simplicial maps as a discrete approximation framework for homotopical complexity in the category of simplicial complexes. We construct an increasing sequence of invariants that approximate the contiguity distance from below. The fundamental properties of m-contiguity distance are established, including invariance under barycentric subdivision, behavior under compositions, and a categorical product inequality. As applications of this theory, we define the m-simplicial Lusternik-Schnirelmann category and the m-discrete topological complexity, proving that each arises naturally as a special case of m-contiguity distance.

</details>


### [182] [Conservative geometric functors via purity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14886)
*Natàlia Castellana,Juan Omar Gómez*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文建立了判断几何函子族何时联合保守的准则，通过紧生成三角范畴中的纯度视角，引入纯可降性概念，并应用于涉及环谱序列极限的两种特定情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在紧生成三角范畴中，需要一种系统方法来判定一族几何函子何时是联合保守的。现有方法在处理环谱的序列极限等几何场景时存在局限性，因此需要发展基于纯度理论的新准则。

Method: 通过引入纯可降性这一新概念，利用紧生成三角范畴中的纯度理论建立判别准则。该方法特别关注几何函子族，并将理论应用于环谱序列极限的两种具体构造。

Result: 建立了纯可降性与几何函子族联合保守性之间的等价关系，为判断函子保守性提供了新的理论工具。在环谱序列极限的两种情形中验证了该准则的有效性。

Conclusion: 纯可降性为判断几何函子族的联合保守性提供了有效的理论框架，特别适用于处理环谱序列极限等几何拓扑学中的问题，扩展了纯度理论在三角范畴中的应用范围。

Abstract: We establish a criterion for determining when a family of geometric functors is jointly conservative through the lens of purity in compactly generated triangulated categories. We introduce the notion of pure descendability and we apply it to two particular situations involving sequential limits of ring spectra.

</details>


### [183] [On the non-existence of almost complex structures on sphere bundles over complex projective spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14945)
*Chengwan Liu,Huijun Yang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 研究复射影空间上偶数维球丛的殆复结构存在性，建立了非存在性的必要条件，并应用于典型线丛的具体族，得到p进赋值判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究复射影空间上偶数维球丛的殆复结构存在性问题，旨在建立系统性的非存在性判据，特别是通过特征类的可除性性质来获得具体条件。

Method: 通过陈类计算和特征类的可除性性质进行分析，建立必要条件：当纤维维数q ≥ a(n)时，全空间E_{n,q}不容许殆复结构。应用于典型线丛的具体族，得到基于p进赋值的非存在性判据。

Result: 建立了明确的函数a(n)，使得当q ≥ a(n)时，丛ξ_{n,q}的全空间E_{n,q}不容许殆复结构。对于典型线丛的具体族，获得了基于p进赋值的非存在性判据，特别对p=2给出了简单的数值界。

Conclusion: 该研究为复射影空间上偶数维球丛的殆复结构存在性提供了系统的非存在性判据，但4 ≤ q < a(n)范围内的存在性问题仍然开放，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: We study the existence of almost complex structures on even-dimensional sphere bundles over complex projective spaces. For bundles $ξ_{n,q}$ with fibre $S^{2q}$ over $\mathbb{C} P^n$, we establish a necessary condition: if $q \ge a(n)$ for an explicit function, then the total space $E_{n,q}$ does not admit an almost complex structure. As an application, we analyse a concrete family associated with the canonical line bundle and obtain non-existence criteria in terms of $p$-adic valuations; for $p=2$ this yields a simple numerical bound. The proofs rely on Chern class computations and divisibility properties of characteristic classes. The results leave open the question of existence in the range $4 \le q < a(n)$.

</details>
